You are reviewing the work of a data agent that has just finished a task. The agent wrote code, ran it, and looked at results. You will see its complete trajectory, together with the task, the specification and documentation it used, and an index of all available data.

Your job is to find the places where the agent's work may be wrong, and to rank them by importance. Do not solve the task, do not write code, and do not judge whether the agent's final answer is correct as a whole.

Look for two kinds of problems:
- Places where the agent's work does not match what the task, specification, or documentation requires: scope and filters, metric definitions, which column a business term refers to, handling of special cases (missing values, zero denominators, invalid values), and the required form, granularity, and type of the output.
- Places where the agent's work may rest on a wrong understanding of the data: what a field or value means, what a row represents, whether a key is unique, whether records match in a join, what nulls mean, units, formats, where counting starts, and the time range or entities the data covers.

Listing a possible problem is not a claim that it is a real error. Many will turn out to be fine; they are listed so that they can be checked later. A possible problem that is unlikely but would change the result a lot if it were real should still be listed, ranked lower.

Two kinds of problems are easy to miss, so check for them explicitly:
- Omitted steps: processing that the specification or documentation requires but the code does not perform.
- Unused materials: files, tables, or columns in the index that bear on the task but that the agent did not use.

Rank problems by importance, combining two things: how likely it is to be a real problem given the materials you can see, and how much the agent's result would change if it is. A possible problem that would change little should rank low.

Return a JSON object whose keys are the names of every table or result the agent produced. For each, give an object with:
- "items": an ordered list of the 10 most important possible problems, most important first. Each item has:
  - "problem": one sentence describing what may be wrong.
  - "about": "requirement" or "data".
  - "evidence_in_work": a short quote from the agent's code or output that shows where the problem is.
  - "source": a short quote from the task, specification, documentation, or index that the problem is based on, or null.
  - "importance": an integer from 1 (low) to 5 (high).
  - "reason": one short sentence explaining the importance.
- "fewer_reason": if you listed fewer than 10 items, explain why; otherwise null.
List fewer than 10 only if you cannot find 10 possible problems for that table or result.

Examples of possible problems (from an unrelated online-shop task; they show the form of an item, not what to look for):
- {"problem": "If the amount column in payments is in cents rather than US dollars, revenue would be off by a factor of 100.", "about": "data", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(p.amount) AS revenue_usd", "source": null, "importance": 2, "reason": "The column name suggests dollars, so this is unlikely, but the effect would be large."}
- {"problem": "Revenue is computed from total_charged, which may include shipping fees, while the specification may define revenue as item price times quantity.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "SUM(o.total_charged) AS revenue_usd", "source": "revenue: sum of item_price * quantity", "importance": 4, "reason": "If the specification's definition applies, every row of revenue would be too high."}
- {"problem": "The specification requires converting created_at to UTC before deriving order_date, but the code takes the date directly.", "about": "requirement", "evidence_in_work": "CAST(o.created_at AS DATE) AS order_date", "source": "convert created_at to UTC before deriving order_date", "importance": 5, "reason": "Orders near midnight would be assigned to the wrong day."}

=== Task ===
## Task Description:
You are a professional Data Engineer responsible for implementing a complete SQL data pipeline based on data contract documents.

## Objectives
Populate the SQL files in the `./sql/` directory according to the specifications in `docs/data_contract.yaml` and `config/layer_dependencies.yaml`. Your task is clear: write the necessary SQL code to ensure `run.py` executes successfully.

You MUST carefully review the content of `docs/data_contract.yaml`. You may use tools to view it. Since the file content is long, you can view it in chunks. Your task is to complete the SQL file writing based on the content of this file.

## Project Background
* Raw data has been loaded into `xxxx_start.duckdb` (Note: 'xxxx' is a placeholder for the project name, such as `greenhouse_start.duckdb` or `google_ads_start.duckdb`; 'xxxx' appearing below carries the same meaning).
* Currently, running `python run.py` completes the data transformation and outputs `xxxx.duckdb`.
* The existing SQL project is stored in the `sql/` folder.
* The current requirements documentation is located in `docs/data_contract.yaml`.

**Implementation Requirements:**
1. **Strictly Adhere to Configuration**:
    * All SQL logic and dependencies must be written strictly according to the `staging`, `intermediate`, and `marts` hierarchy defined in the YAML configuration files.

2. **File and Directory Structure**:
    * SQL files must be created in the corresponding level subdirectories (e.g., `sql/staging/`, `sql/intermediate/`, `sql/marts/`).
    * Strictly follow predefined file naming conventions and coding styles.

3. **Use Pure DuckDB Syntax**:
    * All SQL code must be compatible with DuckDB.
    * **Do NOT** end SQL statements with a semicolon (`;`) at the end of the file.

4. **No dbt Syntax**:
    * You must reference upstream tables directly using the `schema.table_name` format.
    * **Do NOT** use the dbt `ref()` function.

    **Examples**:
    * **Correct ✅**:
```sql
    with account_base as (
    select *
    from staging.stg_salesforce__account
    where not is_deleted
    ),

    account_enriched as (
    select
    account_id,
    ...
```

* **Incorrect ❌**:
```sql
    WITH accounts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__account') }}}}
    ),
    contacts AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {{{{ ref('stg_salesforce__contact') }}}}
    ),
    ...
```
Output Standard: Ensure the generated SQL files can be successfully executed by run.py to produce high-quality data results that meet business requirements.

## Data Contract Documentation (docs/data_contract.yaml):

```yaml
version: "1.0"
metadata:
  description: "Complete data pipeline specification for Zuora source system (Fivetran extracts). This document defines the single source of truth for data cleaning, validation, standardization, and business modeling."
  author: "Data Engineering Team"
  source_system: "Zuora"
  raw_ingest: "Fivetran CSV -> DuckDB"
  pipeline_phases: ["staging_contract", "modeling_spec"]

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# PHASE 1: Defines the transformation from raw data to clean staging tables.  #
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
staging_contract:
  purpose: "Transform raw Zuora-extracted tables into clean, validated, and standardized staging tables. Enforce data quality actions deterministically and surface a 'is_most_recent_record' flag for latest snapshots."
  quality_actions:
    correct:
      description: "Automatically fix deterministic format issues such as email casing, whitespace trimming, phone formatting, and standardized date casts."
      examples: ["LOWER(TRIM(work_email))", "standardize phone format to (XXX) XXX-XXXX", "safe TIMESTAMP casts with try_cast"]
    delete_row:
      description: "Remove entire row when records contain severe data quality issues that make them unusable downstream."
      examples: ["Invalid or out-of-range essential dates (future or pre-2000)", "Account status outside allowed set"]
    nullify_field:
      description: "Set invalid field values to NULL when the field is non-critical and the record can still be useful."
      examples: ["Out-of-range monetary amounts", "Unparsable optional timestamps"]

  tables:
[Per-table sections of the data contract are omitted here. The sections relevant to each review appear under "Relevant specification and documentation".]
modeling_spec:
  purpose: "Transform staging data into business-ready models with clearly defined grains, consistent dependencies, and deterministic business logic."
  design_principles:
    staging_layer_only_references: "Staging models only reference raw tables."
    intermediate_layer_only_references: "Intermediate models only reference staging models."
    marts_layer_only_references: "Marts models only reference intermediate models."
    dependency_rules: "No cross-layer or same-layer circular dependencies."
    grain_definition_required: "Each model must define a single, unambiguous grain."

  intermediate_models:
  marts_models:

```

Important: You can only interact with the provided environment. Do not ask a human for help.

=== Specification and documentation ===
## stg_zuora__account

    - name: stg_zuora__account
      description: "Cleaned and normalized account records; latest snapshot indicator provided."
      source_table: raw.zuora_account_data
      columns:
        - name: account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: account_number
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: auto_pay
          data_type: BOOLEAN
        - name: balance
          data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "try_cast(balance as DECIMAL(18,2)) IS NOT NULL"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "try_cast(balance as DECIMAL(18,2)) BETWEEN -1000000 AND 1000000"
              on_failure: nullify_field
            - rule: "balance = ROUND(CAST(balance AS DECIMAL(18,2)), 2)"
              on_failure: correct
        - name: batch
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: bill_cycle_day
          data_type: INTEGER
        - name: bill_to_contact_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
        - name: credit_balance
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: crm_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: currency
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: default_payment_method_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: last_invoice_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "NULLIF(last_invoice_date,'') IS NULL OR try_cast(last_invoice_date as TIMESTAMP) IS NOT NULL"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: mrr
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: name
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: notes
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: parent_account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: payment_term
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: sold_to_contact_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: status
          data_type: VARCHAR
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "lower(status) IN ('active','draft','cancelled')"
              on_failure: delete_row
            - rule: "status = CASE WHEN lower(status)='active' THEN 'Active' WHEN lower(status)='draft' THEN 'Draft' WHEN lower(status)='cancelled' THEN 'Cancelled' ELSE status END"
              on_failure: correct
            - rule: "status IS NOT NULL"
              on_failure: delete_row
        - name: tax_exempt_effective_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
        - name: tax_exempt_expiration_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
        - name: tax_exempt_status
          data_type: BOOLEAN
        - name: total_debit_memo_balance
          data_type: BIGINT
        - name: total_invoice_balance
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: updated_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: updated_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
        - name: vatid
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: is_most_recent_record
          data_type: BOOLEAN
      row_filters:
        - rule: "_fivetran_deleted IS NULL OR _fivetran_deleted = false"
          on_failure: delete_row



## stg_zuora__invoice

    - name: stg_zuora__invoice
      description: "Cleaned invoices with safe casting and temporal validity checks; latest snapshot indicator provided."
      source_table: raw.zuora_invoice_data
      columns:
        - name: invoice_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
          source_expression: "id"
        - name: account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: adjustment_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
          source_expression: "case when nullif(amount,'') is null then null else cast(amount as DOUBLE) end"
        - name: amount_home_currency
          data_type: DOUBLE
          source_expression: "case when nullif(amount_home_currency,'') is null then null else cast(amount_home_currency as DOUBLE) end"
        - name: amount_without_tax
          data_type: BIGINT
        - name: amount_without_tax_home_currency
          data_type: BIGINT
        - name: auto_pay

          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: bill_to_contact_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: comments
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "case when try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) is null then null when try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) > current_timestamp then null when try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) < timestamp '2000-01-01 00:00:00' then null else cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) end"
        - name: credit_balance_adjustment_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: credit_memo_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: default_payment_method_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: due_date
          data_type: DATE
          source_expression: "try_cast(due_date as DATE)"
        - name: exchange_rate
          data_type: DOUBLE
        - name: exchange_rate_date
          data_type: DATE
        - name: home_currency
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: includes_one_time
          data_type: BOOLEAN
        - name: includes_recurring
          data_type: BOOLEAN
        - name: includes_usage
          data_type: BOOLEAN
        - name: invoice_date
          data_type: DATE
          source_expression: "try_cast(invoice_date as DATE)"
        - name: invoice_number
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: last_email_sent_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "case when nullif(last_email_sent_date,'') is null then null else cast(last_email_sent_date as TIMESTAMP) end"
        - name: parent_account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: payment_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
          source_expression: "case when nullif(payment_amount,'') is null then null else cast(payment_amount as DOUBLE) end"
        - name: payment_term
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: posted_by
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: posted_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "cast(posted_date as TIMESTAMP)"
        - name: refund_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
          source_expression: "case when nullif(refund_amount,'') is null then null else cast(refund_amount as DOUBLE) end"
        - name: sold_to_contact_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: source
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: source_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: source_type
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: status
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: target_date
          data_type: DATE
        - name: tax_amount
          data_type: DOUBLE
          source_expression: "case when nullif(tax_amount,'') is null then null else cast(tax_amount as DOUBLE) end"
        - name: tax_status
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: transaction_currency
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: transferred_to_accounting
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: updated_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: updated_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "cast(updated_date as TIMESTAMP)"
        - name: is_most_recent_record
          data_type: BOOLEAN
          source_expression: "row_number() over (partition by id order by try_cast(updated_date as TIMESTAMP) desc) = 1"
      row_filters:
        - rule: "_fivetran_deleted IS NULL OR _fivetran_deleted = false"
          on_failure: delete_row
        - rule: "try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) is null OR (try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) <= current_timestamp AND try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) >= timestamp '2000-01-01 00:00:00')"
          on_failure: delete_row



## stg_zuora__contact

    - name: stg_zuora__contact
      description: "Cleaned contacts with email normalization and phone format standardization; latest per contact and per account-contact flags."
      source_table: raw.zuora_contact_data
      columns:
        - name: contact_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
          source_expression: "id"
        - name: account_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: address_1
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: address_2
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: city
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: country
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: created_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP)"
        - name: description
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: first_name
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: last_name
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: postal_code
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: state
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: updated_by_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: updated_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "cast(updated_date as TIMESTAMP)"
        - name: work_email
          data_type: VARCHAR
          source_expression: "lower(trim(work_email))"
          validation_rules:
            - rule: "work_email IS NULL OR work_email RLIKE '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$'"
              on_failure: nullify_field
        - name: work_phone
          data_type: VARCHAR
        - name: mobile_phone
          data_type: VARCHAR
          source_expression: "case when mobile_phone is null then null else (case when length(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', '')) = 10 then '(' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),1,3) || ') ' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),4,3) || '-' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),7,4) when length(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', '')) = 11 then '(' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),2,3) || ') ' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),5,3) || '-' || substr(regexp_replace(mobile_phone, '[^0-9]', ''),8,4) else null end) end"
        - name: is_most_recent_record
          data_type: BOOLEAN
          source_expression: "row_number() over (partition by id order by try_cast(updated_date as TIMESTAMP) desc) = 1"
        - name: is_most_recent_account_contact
          data_type: BOOLEAN
          source_expression: "row_number() over (partition by account_id order by try_cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP) desc) = 1"
      row_filters:
        - rule: "_fivetran_deleted IS NULL OR _fivetran_deleted = false"
          on_failure: delete_row

## stg_zuora__product_rate_plan

    - name: stg_zuora__product_rate_plan
      description: "Cleaned product rate plans; latest snapshot indicator."
      source_table: raw.zuora_product_rate_plan_data
      columns:
        - name: product_rate_plan_id
          data_type: VARCHAR
          source_expression: "id"

        - name: created_date
          data_type: TIMESTAMP
          source_expression: "cast(created_date as TIMESTAMP)"

          data_type: DATE
        - name: effective_start_date
          data_type: DATE

## int_zuora__account_current

    - name: int_zuora__account_current
      description: Pass-through of staging.stg_zuora__account rows flagged as most recent; all source columns preserved.
      grain: One row per staging.stg_zuora__account row where is_most_recent_record is TRUE
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__account
      business_logic: '- Input: all rows from staging.stg_zuora__account. - Filter: keep only rows where is_most_recent_record is TRUE; rows where it is FALSE or NULL are excluded. - Projection: select all columns exactly as in the source; no transformations, casts, or recalculations. - Deduplication: none; if multiple source rows are flagged TRUE for the same account_id, all are preserved. - Data fidelity: NULLs, casing, and whitespace are preserved exactly as stored in the source. - Ordering: not specified; downstream consumers must not rely on row order. - Result invariant: is_most_recent_record is always TRUE in this table.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Zuora account identifier from staging
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: account_number
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Zuora account number as in staging
        tests: []
      - name: auto_pay
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Auto-pay flag as ingested (text)
        tests: []
      - name: balance
        data_type: DECIMAL(18,2)
        description: Account balance passthrough with two decimal places
        tests: []
      - name: batch
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Billing batch code as in staging
        tests: []
      - name: bill_cycle_day
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Bill cycle day as text from staging
        tests: []
      - name: bill_to_contact_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Bill-to contact ID
        tests: []
      - name: created_by_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Creator user ID
        tests: []
      - name: created_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Account creation timestamp
        tests: []
      - name: credit_balance
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Credit balance as ingested (text)
        tests: []
      - name: crm_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Upstream CRM account ID
        tests: []
      - name: currency
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account currency code as in staging
        tests: []
      - name: default_payment_method_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Default payment method ID
        tests: []
      - name: last_invoice_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Last invoice date as in staging
        tests: []
      - name: mrr
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Monthly recurring revenue as ingested (text)
        tests: []
      - name: name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name
        tests: []
      - name: notes
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Freeform account notes
        tests: []
      - name: parent_account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Parent account ID
        tests: []
      - name: payment_term
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Payment term code or description
        tests: []
      - name: sold_to_contact_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Sold-to contact ID
        tests: []
      - name: status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account status as in staging
        tests: []
      - name: tax_exempt_effective_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Tax exemption effective date
        tests: []
      - name: tax_exempt_expiration_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Tax exemption expiration date
        tests: []
      - name: tax_exempt_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Tax exemption status
        tests: []
      - name: total_debit_memo_balance
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Total debit memo balance as ingested (text)
        tests: []
      - name: total_invoice_balance
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Total invoice balance as ingested (text)
        tests: []
      - name: updated_by_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last updater user ID
        tests: []

        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Last update timestamp
        tests: []
      - name: vatid

## int_zuora__invoice_base

    - name: int_zuora__invoice_base
      description: Invoice headers from the latest Zuora records with type casts and explicit past-due logic
      grain: One row per invoice_id from stg_zuora__invoice where is_most_recent_record = true
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice
      business_logic: '- Filter to rows where is_most_recent_record is true. - Cast created_date to TIMESTAMP as created_at; cast due_date to DATE; invoice_date is passed through. - Map fields: invoice_amount = amount; invoice_amount_home_currency = amount_home_currency; invoice_amount_paid = payment_amount; purchase_type = source_type. - Compute invoice_amount_unpaid as amount minus payment_amount; if either input is NULL, the result is NULL; can be negative if payments exceed the amount. - Pass through tax_amount, refund_amount, transaction_currency, home_currency, exchange_rate_date, status, account_id, invoice_number. - Compute total_amount_past_due using today''s calendar date (time ignored): - If today is strictly after due_date and amount differs from payment_amount, set total_amount_past_due to balance; otherwise set to 0.0. - If due_date is NULL, or amount or payment_amount is NULL, treat as not past due → 0.0. - If past due and balance is NULL, total_amount_past_due is NULL. - No aggregation or sorting; output is a direct row-wise transform of the filtered source.'
      columns:
      - name: invoice_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unique invoice identifier; one row per latest invoice_id
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account linked to the invoice
        tests: []
      - name: created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Timestamp derived from created_date
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_number
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Human-readable invoice number
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Invoice date from source
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total invoice amount in transaction currency
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_amount_home_currency
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total invoice amount in home currency
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_amount_paid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Amount paid to date in transaction currency
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_amount_unpaid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Amount minus payment_amount; NULL if either is NULL; may be negative
        tests: []
      - name: tax_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Tax amount in transaction currency
        tests: []
      - name: refund_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Refund amount in transaction currency
        tests: []
      - name: transaction_currency
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: ISO currency of transaction amounts
        tests: []
      - name: home_currency
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: ISO home/reporting currency
        tests: []
      - name: exchange_rate_date
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Exchange rate date from source as text
        tests: []
      - name: due_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Invoice due date cast to DATE
        tests: []
      - name: status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice status from source
        tests: []
      - name: purchase_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Purchase type from source_type
        tests: []
      - name: total_amount_past_due
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: 0.0 unless today is after due_date and amount differs from payment_amount, then equals balance; NULL if balance is NULL when past due
        tests: []


## int_zuora__invoice_item_base

    - name: int_zuora__invoice_item_base
      description: Invoice line items filtered to latest records, with charge/service date buckets and discount amounts derived when processing_type='1'.
      grain: One row per source record where is_most_recent_record = TRUE
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item and keep only rows where is_most_recent_record is true. - Pass through all source fields unchanged. - Derive charge_day, charge_week, charge_month from charge_date: - charge_day is the calendar day; charge_week is the start date of the ISO week (Monday); charge_month is the first day of the calendar month. - If charge_date is null, all charge buckets are null. - Derive service_start_day, service_start_week, service_start_month from service_start_date with the same rules and null handling. - Compute discounts: - When processing_type equals the string ''1'', discount_amount_home_currency is the numeric value of charge_amount_home_currency and discount_amount is the numeric value of charge_amount. - When processing_type is not ''1'', both discount fields are set to 0.0. - If processing_type is ''1'' and the source amount is null, the corresponding discount field is null. No rounding or sign changes are applied. - No aggregation, deduplication, or ordering is performed; one output row per filtered source row.'

## int_zuora__billing_enriched

    - name: int_zuora__billing_enriched
      description: Per-invoice aggregates from most-recent invoice items, taxes, payments, and credit balance adjustments
      grain: One row per invoice_id present in most-recent invoice items
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice_payment
      - staging.stg_zuora__payment
      - staging.stg_zuora__credit_balance_adjustment
      - staging.stg_zuora__taxation_item
      business_logic: '- Keep only rows marked as most recent in each staging source. - Aggregate invoice items by invoice_id: - invoice_items: number of unique invoice_item_id; NULLs ignored. - products: number of unique product_id; NULLs ignored. - subscriptions: number of unique subscription_id; NULLs ignored. - discount_charges: total charge_amount for items where processing_type equals ''1'' (string compare); items not matching contribute 0; result is NULL if all qualifying amounts are NULL. - discount_charges_home_currency: same logic using charge_amount_home_currency. - units: total of quantity; NULLs ignored; NULL if all quantities are NULL. - first_charge_date: earliest charge_date; NULLs ignored. - most_recent_charge_date: latest charge_date; NULLs ignored. - invoice_service_start_date: earliest service_start_date; NULLs ignored. - invoice_service_end_date: latest service_end_date; NULLs ignored. - Taxes: per invoice_id, total tax_amount_home_currency across taxation items; NULLs ignored; NULL if no taxation items or all amounts are NULL. - Payments: join invoice_payment to payment on payment_id, then aggregate per invoice_id: - payments: number of unique payment_id; NULLs ignored. - invoice_amount_paid_home_currency: total of amount_home_currency with NULL or non-numeric treated as 0; NULL if no matched payments exist. - first_payment_date: earliest effective_date string (by string order); NULLs ignored; NULL if none. - most_recent_payment_date: latest effective_date string (by string order); NULLs ignored; NULL if none. - payment_methods: number of unique payment_method_id; NULLs ignored; 0 if all method IDs are NULL; NULL if no matched payments exist. - Credit balance adjustments: per invoice_id aggregate: - credit_balance_adjustments: number of unique credit_balance_adjustment_id; NULLs ignored. - credit_balance_adjustment_amount_home_currency: total of amount_home_currency; NULLs ignored; NULL if none or all amounts are NULL. - first_credit_balance_adjustment_date: earliest adjustment_date; NULLs ignored; NULL if none. - most_recent_credit_balance_adjustment_date: latest adjustment_date; NULLs ignored; NULL if none. - Final output starts from invoices with at least one most-recent invoice item and left joins taxes, payments, and credit aggregates on invoice_id; unmatched joins produce NULLs.'
      columns:
      - name: invoice_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice identifier; one row per invoice_id from most-recent invoice items
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: invoice_items
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique invoice_item_id count from most-recent invoice items; NULLs ignored
        tests: []
      - name: products
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique product_id count from most-recent invoice items; NULLs ignored
        tests: []
      - name: subscriptions
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique subscription_id count from most-recent invoice items; NULLs ignored
        tests: []
      - name: discount_charges
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total charge_amount for items where processing_type='1'; non-matching items add 0; NULL if all qualifying amounts are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: discount_charges_home_currency
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total charge_amount_home_currency for items where processing_type='1'; non-matching items add 0; NULL if all qualifying amounts are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: units
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total quantity across items; NULLs ignored; NULL if all quantities are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: first_charge_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Earliest non-null charge_date across items; NULL if all are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: most_recent_charge_date
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Latest non-null charge_date across items; NULL if all are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_service_start_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Earliest non-null service_start_date across items; NULL if all are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_service_end_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Latest non-null service_end_date across items; NULL if all are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: tax_amount_home_currency
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total tax_amount_home_currency from taxation items; NULL if none or all amounts are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: payments
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique payment_id count from payments linked via invoice_payment; NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: invoice_amount_paid_home_currency
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total of payment amounts in home currency with NULL or non-numeric treated as 0; NULL if no linked payments
        tests: []
      - name: first_payment_date
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Earliest effective_date string among linked payments (string order); NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: most_recent_payment_date
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Latest effective_date string among linked payments (string order); NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: payment_methods
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique payment_method_id count across linked payments; 0 if all method IDs are NULL; NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: credit_balance_adjustments
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Unique credit_balance_adjustment_id count for the invoice_id; NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: credit_balance_adjustment_amount_home_currency
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total amount_home_currency across credit balance adjustments; NULL if none or all amounts are NULL
        tests: []
      - name: first_credit_balance_adjustment_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Earliest non-null adjustment_date across credit balance adjustments; NULL if none
        tests: []
      - name: most_recent_credit_balance_adjustment_date
        data_type: DATE
        description: Latest non-null adjustment_date across credit balance adjustments; NULL if none
        tests: []


## int_zuora__transactions_grouped

    - name: int_zuora__transactions_grouped
      description: Daily Zuora invoice-based transaction aggregates per account with day/week/month/year buckets.
      grain: One row per account_id per date_day
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
      business_logic: '- Keep only records flagged as most recent from both invoices and invoice items. - Invoices use: invoice_id, account_id, invoice_date, amount, payment_amount, refund_amount. - Invoice items use: invoice_id, invoice_item_id. - For each invoice, count unique invoice_item_id values to get its item count; null item IDs do not contribute. - Left join per-invoice item counts to invoices by invoice_id so invoices without items are retained. - Derive calendar buckets from invoice_date: - date_day: the calendar date (time removed). - date_week: Monday of the week containing the date. - date_month: first day of the calendar month. - date_year: first day of the calendar year. - Group by account_id and the four time buckets (equivalent to account_id by date_day) and compute: - daily_invoices: number of unique invoices in the group; missing invoice IDs do not count. - daily_invoice_items: sum of each invoice’s unique item count; invoices with no items contribute 0; if none have items the result is 0. - daily_invoice_amount: total of invoice amounts; null amounts are ignored; result is null if no non-null amounts exist in the group. - daily_invoice_amount_paid: total of payment amounts; nulls are ignored; result is null if all are null. - daily_invoice_amount_unpaid: for each invoice take amount minus payment amount, then total; if either value is null for an invoice it contributes nothing; result is null if all are null. - daily_refunds: total of refund amounts; nulls are ignored; result is null if all are null. - Output one row per account_id and date_day; other date buckets are deterministic functions of invoice_date for that row.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Zuora account identifier used to group aggregates.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar date from invoice_date with time removed.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_week
        data_type: DATE
        description: Monday of the week containing invoice_date.
      - name: date_month
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the month containing invoice_date.
      - name: date_year
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the year containing invoice_date.
      - name: daily_invoices
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct invoice count for the account and date_day; missing invoice IDs do not count.
      - name: daily_invoice_items
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Sum of per-invoice unique item counts; invoices with no items contribute 0 and result is 0 if none have items.
      - name: daily_invoice_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total invoice amounts for the group; null amounts are ignored and result is null if all are null.
      - name: daily_invoice_amount_paid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total payment amounts for the group; nulls are ignored and result is null if all are null.
      - name: daily_invoice_amount_unpaid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total of per-invoice (amount minus payment); invoices with null in either value contribute nothing; result is null if all are null.
      - name: daily_refunds
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total refund amounts for the group; nulls are ignored and result is null if all are null.


## int_zuora__transaction_date_spine

    - name: int_zuora__transaction_date_spine
      description: Continuous daily date spine from Zuora invoices with week/month/year buckets and a sequential index.
      grain: One row per calendar day between min and max invoice_date (inclusive)
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice (invoice_date, is_most_recent_record)
      business_logic: '- Start from staging.stg_zuora__invoice and keep only rows where is_most_recent_record is true. - Determine bounds: - min_date = the minimum invoice_date truncated to the calendar day. - max_date = the maximum invoice_date truncated to the calendar day. - Null invoice_date values do not affect these bounds; if no qualifying rows exist, both bounds are null. - Generate a continuous daily series from min_date to max_date inclusive with a 1-day step; if either bound is null, return no rows. - For each generated day: - date_day = that calendar day. - date_week = the start of the week for date_day (Monday). - date_month = the first day of the month for date_day. - date_year = the first day of the year for date_day. - date_index = a 1-based sequential counter ordered by date_day ascending, contiguous with no gaps.'
      columns:
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar day generated from the inclusive min and max invoice_date bounds.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: date_week
        data_type: DATE
        description: Start of the week (Monday) for date_day.
        tests: []
      - name: date_month
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the month for date_day.
        tests: []
      - name: date_year
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the year for date_day.
        tests: []
      - name: date_index
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: 1-based sequential index by date_day ascending; strictly increasing and unique.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique

## int_zuora__mrr_date_spine

    - name: int_zuora__mrr_date_spine
      description: Continuous daily spine from the minimum to maximum day-level service_start_date among most recent Zuora invoice items.
      grain: One row per date_day
      source_models:
      - staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item (service_start_date, is_most_recent_record)
      business_logic: '- Filter the invoice items to rows marked as the most recent record. - Determine the earliest and latest service_start_date after truncating to the day. - Generate a consecutive daily series from the earliest to the latest day, inclusive. - For each day in the series: - date_day: the calendar day (date only). - date_week: the first day of that week, with weeks starting on Monday (DuckDB default). - date_month: the first day of that month. - date_year: the first day of that year. - date_index: assign 1 to the earliest day and increment by 1 for each subsequent day in ascending order, with no gaps. - The output has one unique row per date_day, ordered ascending. - Null policy: - Null service_start_date values are ignored when determining bounds. - If no non-null service_start_date exists after filtering, the result contains no rows.'
      columns:
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar day from the generated spine; unique and ascending.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: date_week
        data_type: DATE
        description: Start date of the week (Monday) for the given date_day.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_month
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the month corresponding to date_day.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_year
        data_type: DATE
        description: First day of the year corresponding to date_day.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_index
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Sequential 1-based index by ascending date_day; strictly consecutive without gaps.
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique


## zuora__account_overview

    - name: zuora__account_overview
      description: Account-level overview combining current account fields, current contact fields, invoice aggregates, and invoice item counts
      grain: One row per account_id present in int_zuora__account_current
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_zuora__account_current
      - intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current
      - intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base
      - intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base
      - intermediate.int_zuora__billing_enriched
      business_logic: '- Base: Start from intermediate.int_zuora__account_current (assumed one current row per account_id). - Contact: Left join intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current on account_id; if no contact row, all contact fields are NULL. - Invoice item count: - Build items_by_account by joining intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base to intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base on invoice_id, then counting distinct invoice_item_id per account_id. - Left join items_by_account to accounts. The count reflects distinct item IDs across all invoices of the account. If an account has invoices but none have items, the count is 0. If an account has no invoices, there is no items_by_account row and the joined count is NULL. - Invoice aggregates: - Build per_invoice by left joining intermediate.int_zuora__billing_enriched to intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base on invoice_id, then grouping by account_id to compute: - first_charge_date: earliest non-NULL first_charge_date across the account’s invoices. - total_amount_paid: sum of invoice_amount_paid. - total_amount_not_paid: sum of invoice_amount_unpaid. - total_taxes: sum of tax_amount. - total_invoice_amount: sum of invoice_amount. - Summations skip NULL inputs per row; if an account has invoices but all values for a summed field are NULL, the result is NULL. If an account has no invoices, no per_invoice row exists and all joined aggregates are NULL. - Invoice count: - Compute per account_id using a correlated subquery over intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base that counts distinct invoices; returns 0 when no invoices exist. - Output: - Select pass-through account fields, joined contact fields, per_invoice.first_charge_date as first_charge_processed_at, items_by_account.invoice_item_count, the scalar invoice_count, and the per_invoice totals. No zero-coalescing, rounding, or casting; all joins from accounts are left joins.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account identifier from int_zuora__account_current; exactly one output row per account_id
        tests:
        - not_null
        - unique
      - name: account_created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Account created_date from int_zuora__account_current
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from int_zuora__account_current
      - name: account_number
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account number from int_zuora__account_current
      - name: account_credit_balance
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Raw credit_balance from int_zuora__account_current (string as stored; no casting)
      - name: account_zuora_calculated_mrr
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Raw mrr from int_zuora__account_current (Zuora-calculated MRR; string as stored)
      - name: account_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account status from int_zuora__account_current
      - name: is_auto_pay
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Raw auto_pay flag from int_zuora__account_current (string as stored)
      - name: account_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Country from int_zuora__contact_current.country (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: City from int_zuora__contact_current.city (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Work email from int_zuora__contact_current.work_email (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First name from int_zuora__contact_current.first_name (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last name from int_zuora__contact_current.last_name (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_postal_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Postal code from int_zuora__contact_current.postal_code (NULL if no contact)
      - name: account_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: State from int_zuora__contact_current.state (NULL if no contact)
      - name: first_charge_processed_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Earliest non-NULL first_charge_date from billing_enriched across all invoices for the account; NULL if no invoices or all values NULL
      - name: invoice_item_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of invoice_item_id across all invoices for the account; 0 if invoices exist but no items; NULL if the account has no invoices
      - name: invoice_count
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Distinct count of invoices for the account; returns 0 when no invoices exist
      - name: total_taxes
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of tax_amount across all invoices for the account; NULL if no invoices or if all values are NULL
      - name: total_invoice_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of invoice_amount across all invoices for the account; NULL if no invoices or if all values are NULL
      - name: total_amount_paid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of invoice_amount_paid across all invoices for the account; NULL if no invoices or if all values are NULL
      - name: total_amount_not_paid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of invoice_amount_unpaid across all invoices for the account; NULL if no invoices or if all values are NULL

## zuora__line_item_history

    - name: zuora__line_item_history
      description: Historical invoice line items enriched with invoice, product, rate plan charge, subscription, and amendment attributes from current snapshots.
      grain: One row per invoice_item_id; duplicates may occur if joined tables have multiple matches.
      source_models:
      - int_zuora__invoice_item_base
      - int_zuora__invoice_base
      - int_zuora__product_current
      - int_zuora__product_rate_plan_current
      - int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current

      - int_zuora__subscription_current
      - int_zuora__amendment_current
      business_logic: '- Start with all rows from int_zuora__invoice_item_base (alias ii); the row grain is the line item (invoice_item_id); no aggregation or de-duplication. - Left join int_zuora__invoice_base (inv) on ii.invoice_id = inv.invoice_id; select: - inv.created_at → account_created_at - inv.status → account_status - inv.invoice_number, inv.invoice_date, inv.due_date → invoice_due_date - Left join int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current (rpc) on ii.rate_plan_charge_id = rpc.rate_plan_charge_id; select: - rpc.name → rate_plan_charge_name - rpc.billing_period → charge_billing_period - rpc.billing_timing → charge_billing_timing - rpc.charge_model → charge_model - rpc.charge_type → charge_type - rpc.effective_start_date → charge_effective_start_date - rpc.effective_end_date → charge_effective_end_date - rpc.segment → charge_segment - rpc.mrr → charge_mrr - rpc.mrrhome_currency → charge_mrr_home_currency - Left join int_zuora__amendment_current (amd) on ii.amendment_id = amd.amendment_id; select amd.name → amendment_name, amd.type → amendment_type, amd.status → amendment_status. - Left join int_zuora__product_current (prod) on ii.product_id = prod.product_id; select prod.name → product_name, prod.category → product_category, prod.description → product_description, prod.effective_start_date → product_start_date, prod.effective_end_date → product_end_date. - Left join int_zuora__product_rate_plan_current (prp) on ii.product_rate_plan_id = prp.product_rate_plan_id; select prp.name → product_rate_plan_name, prp.description → product_rate_plan_description. - Left join int_zuora__rate_plan_current (rate_plan) on ii.rate_plan_id = rate_plan.rate_plan_id; no fields selected. - Left join int_zuora__subscription_current (sub) on ii.subscription_id = sub.subscription_id; no fields selected. - All joins are LEFT JOINs (no row filtering). If a joined table has multiple rows per key, the output will fan out accordingly; duplicates are preserved. - Columns from joined tables are NULL when no match exists; no defaulting, casting, rounding, or recomputation is applied. - All ii.* columns are passed through exactly as stored in int_zuora__invoice_item_base; date truncation fields (charge_day/charge_week/charge_month, service_start_day/service_start_week/service_start_month) are used as provided.'

## zuora__line_item_enhanced

    - name: zuora__line_item_enhanced
      description: Zuora invoice line items enriched with invoice, payment, product, subscription, and customer attributes; duplicates can occur from one-to-many joins
      grain: One row per joined combination of a line item with matching invoice_payment, contact, and rate_plan rows for its invoice/account/subscription
      source_models:
      - int_zuora__invoice_item_base
      - int_zuora__account_current
      - int_zuora__contact_current
      - int_zuora__invoice_base
      - int_zuora__invoice_payment_current
      - int_zuora__payment_current
      - int_zuora__payment_method_current
      - int_zuora__product_current
      - int_zuora__subscription_current
      - int_zuora__rate_plan_current
      business_logic: 'Start from invoice line items. Enrich via LEFT JOINs: - Invoices by invoice_id to bring created_at, status, purchase_type, refund_amount. - Invoice payments by invoice_id to bring payment_id and payment_method_id. - Payments by payment_id to bring effective_date and the related payment_method_id. - Payment methods by the payment''s payment_method_id to bring payment_method_name. - Accounts by account_id to bring account created_date and name. - Contacts by account_id to bring first_name, last_name, work_email, city, country; all matching contacts are retained (no primary-contact filter). - Products by product_id to bring product name and category. - Subscriptions by subscription_id to bring start/end dates (converted to timestamp) and status. - Rate plans by subscription_id to bring rate_plan name. Multiplicity and duplicates: - Multiple rows can result per line item when an invoice has multiple payments, an account has multiple contacts, and/or a subscription has multiple rate plans; these multiplicities combine across those dimensions. Derived/transform rules: - line_item_index: assign a 1-based sequence within each invoice, ordered by line_item_id ascending across the final result; order among identical line_item_id values is non-deterministic. - transaction_type: interpret processing_type as a string where ''0''→charge, ''1''→discount, ''2''→prepayment, ''3''→tax; otherwise NULL. - discount_amount: for discount rows (processing_type=''1''), set to charge_amount converted to double; otherwise 0.0; if charge_amount is NULL on a discount row, the result is NULL. - customer_level: constant value ''customer''. - customer_name: concatenate contact first name, a single space, and last name; if either name part is NULL, the result is NULL; no trimming or case changes. - Subscription period fields: convert subscription start and end dates to timestamp. Pass-through fields (carried as stored, no normalization): quantity, unit_amount (unit_price), tax_amount, total_amount (charge_amount), currency, payment_at (payments.effective_date). Null policy: - All joins are LEFT joins from line items; unmatched enrichments yield NULLs. - Pass-through fields preserve NULLs from the source.'
      columns:
      - name: header_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice header id from line_items.invoice_id
      - name: line_item_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice item id from line_items.invoice_item_id
      - name: line_item_index
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: 1-based sequence within each header_id ordered by line_item_id ascending across the final result; order among identical line_item_id values is non-deterministic
      - name: line_item_created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Creation timestamp from line_items.created_date
      - name: invoice_created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Invoice creation timestamp from invoices.created_at
      - name: header_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice status from invoices.status
      - name: billing_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Invoice purchase type from invoices.purchase_type
      - name: currency
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Transaction currency from line_items.transaction_currency (verbatim)
      - name: product_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Product id from line_items.product_id
      - name: product_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Product name from products.name; NULL if no product match
      - name: product_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Product category from products.category; NULL if no product match
      - name: transaction_type
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Processing type mapped to charge, discount, prepayment, or tax; NULL if unmapped
      - name: quantity
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Quantity from line_items.quantity (verbatim string; NULLs preserved)
      - name: unit_amount
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Unit price from line_items.unit_price (verbatim string; NULLs preserved)
      - name: discount_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: For discount rows set to charge_amount as DOUBLE, otherwise 0.0; NULL if charge_amount is NULL on a discount row
      - name: tax_amount
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Tax amount from line_items.tax_amount (verbatim string; NULLs preserved)
      - name: total_amount
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Total/charge amount from line_items.charge_amount (verbatim string; NULLs preserved)
      - name: payment_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Payment id from invoice_payments.payment_id; NULL if no related invoice_payment
      - name: payment_method_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Payment method id from invoice_payments.payment_method_id; NULL if no related invoice_payment
      - name: payment_method
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Payment method name from payment_methods.payment_method_name via the joined payment; NULL if no related payment or method
      - name: payment_at
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Payment effective date/time from payments.effective_date (verbatim string; NULL if no related payment)
      - name: refund_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Refund amount from invoices.refund_amount; NULL if no invoice match
      - name: subscription_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Subscription id from line_items.subscription_id
      - name: subscription_plan
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Rate plan name from rate_plan.name; duplicates occur if multiple rate plans per subscription
      - name: subscription_period_started_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Subscription start timestamp converted from subscriptions.subscription_start_date
      - name: subscription_period_ended_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Subscription end timestamp converted from subscriptions.subscription_end_date
      - name: subscription_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Subscription status from subscriptions.status; NULL if no subscription match
      - name: customer_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Customer account id from line_items.account_id
      - name: customer_level
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Constant 'customer'
      - name: customer_created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Customer account creation timestamp from accounts.created_date
      - name: customer_company
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Customer company/account name from accounts.name
      - name: customer_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Full name from contact first and last name separated by a space; NULL if either name part is NULL; no trimming
      - name: customer_email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Customer email from contacts.work_email; NULL if no contact match
      - name: customer_city
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Customer city from contacts.city; NULL if no contact match
      - name: customer_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Customer country from contacts.country; NULL if no contact match


## zuora__monthly_recurring_revenue

    - name: zuora__monthly_recurring_revenue
      description: Monthly sums of recurring vs non-recurring invoice amounts per account using the current rate plan charge type.
      grain: One row per account_id and account_month (month of service_start_day)
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base
      - intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current
      business_logic: '- Inputs: - From intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base: account_id, service_start_day, rate_plan_charge_id, charge_amount, discount_amount. - From intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current: rate_plan_charge_id, charge_type. - Build the month spine as distinct pairs of (account_id, month of service_start_day set to the first day). - Left join invoice items to the month spine on account_id and month; then left join the current rate plan charge on rate_plan_charge_id. - Classification for each invoice item: - Recurring (MRR): charge_type equals "recurring" when compared case-insensitively (no trimming). - Non-MRR: charge_type is present and, case-insensitively, not equal to "recurring". - If the rate plan charge is missing or charge_type is NULL, the item contributes 0 to both categories. - Per-line numeric handling: - charge_amount is converted to a DOUBLE; invalid or NULL values are treated as 0.0. - discount_amount treats NULL as 0.0; signs are preserved; no rounding or currency conversion. - If the rate plan charge join returns multiple matches for a line, that line’s amounts are counted multiple times. - Aggregations per (account_id, account_month): - Gross MRR: sum of charge_amount over recurring items. - Discount MRR: sum of discount_amount over recurring items. - Net MRR: sum over recurring items of (charge_amount minus discount_amount). - Gross non-MRR: sum of charge_amount over non-MRR items. - Discount non-MRR: sum of discount_amount over non-MRR items. - Net non-MRR: sum over non-MRR items of (charge_amount minus discount_amount). - Output includes every (account_id, account_month) from the month spine; if no items match a category, the corresponding sums are 0.0.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account identifier used for grouping.
      - name: account_month
        data_type: DATE
        description: Month derived from service_start_day, set to the first day of that month.
      - name: gross_current_month_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of charge_amount (invalid or NULL treated as 0.0) for items classified as recurring (case-insensitive match to 'recurring').
      - name: discount_current_month_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of discount_amount (NULL treated as 0.0) for items classified as recurring.
      - name: net_current_month_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum over recurring items of charge_amount minus discount_amount with the same null handling.
      - name: gross_current_month_non_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of charge_amount (invalid or NULL treated as 0.0) for items where charge_type is present and not 'recurring' (case-insensitive).
      - name: discount_current_month_non_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum of discount_amount (NULL treated as 0.0) for items where charge_type is present and not 'recurring' (case-insensitive).
      - name: net_current_month_non_mrr
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Sum over non-MRR items of charge_amount minus discount_amount with the same null handling.


## zuora__subscription_overview

    - name: zuora__subscription_overview
      description: Subscription snapshot joined to rate plan charges, amendments, and account name; no aggregation; possible multiple rows per subscription due to joins
      grain: One row per subscription_id, rate_plan_charge_id, and amendment_id combination
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_zuora__subscription_current

      - intermediate.int_zuora__amendment_current

      business_logic: '- Start from intermediate.int_zuora__subscription_current and include the listed subscription attributes with no filtering or transformations. - Left join to intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current on subscription_id; include rate_plan_charge_id; rename name to rate_plan_charge_name, billing_period to charge_billing_period, billing_timing to charge_billing_timing, effective_start_date to charge_effective_start_date, effective_end_date to charge_effective_end_date, mrr to charge_mrr, and mrrhome_currency to charge_mrr_home_currency; also include charge_model, charge_type, and charged_through_date. - Left join to intermediate.int_zuora__amendment_current on subscription_id; include amendment_id; rename name to amendment_name, booking_date to amendment_booking_date, created_date to amendment_creation_date, type to amendment_type, status to amendment_status, contract_effective_date to amendment_contract_date, and service_activation_date to amendment_activation_date. - Left join to intermediate.int_zuora__account_current on account_id to add account_name (from name). - Join types are LEFT; if no matching charge exists, all charge-related fields are NULL; if no matching amendment exists, all amendment-related fields are NULL; if no matching account exists, account_name is NULL. - No aggregation, deduplication, or ordering is applied; multiple matching charges and amendments per subscription produce a Cartesian product of those matches. - Values are passed through as stored in sources with original types and formatting; no casting, trimming, case changes, rounding, or unit conversion.'

## zuora__account_daily_overview

    - name: zuora__account_daily_overview
      description: Daily account-level overview joining transaction-day aggregates with a date index and the latest account/contact profile.
      grain: One row per account_id per date_day that exists in both int_zuora__transactions_grouped and int_zuora__transaction_date_spine; if multiple overview rows exist for an account_id, the daily row is duplicated.
      source_models:
      - intermediate.int_zuora__transactions_grouped
      - intermediate.int_zuora__transaction_date_spine
      - intermediate.int_zuora__account_current
      - intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current
      - intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base
      business_logic: '- Start with intermediate.int_zuora__transactions_grouped (rt): for each account_id and date_day it provides date_day, date_week, date_month, date_year, and daily metrics (daily_invoices, daily_invoice_items, daily_invoice_amount, daily_invoice_amount_paid, daily_invoice_amount_unpaid, daily_refunds). These are passed through unchanged; NULLs are preserved and no rounding/conversion is applied. - Join rt to intermediate.int_zuora__transaction_date_spine (spine) on date_day using an inner join to attach spine.date_index. Only rows whose date_day exists in the spine are kept; date_index is the spine’s monotonic day index for that date. - Build an account overview (o): - From intermediate.int_zuora__account_current (a): select account_id, created_date (as account_created_at), name (account_name), account_number, and status (account_status). - Left join intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current (c) on account_id with c.is_most_recent_account_contact = TRUE to attach contact fields: country (account_country), lower(work_email) (account_email), first_name, last_name, postal_code, state. If no such contact exists, these fields are NULL. Email is lowercased exactly; other fields are passed through without trimming or case changes. - Left join intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base (i) on account_id and compute first_charge_processed_at as the earliest invoice_date across all invoices for the account; if no invoices exist, this field is NULL. - Group by all non-aggregated fields to produce one row per distinct account/profile combination. If multiple contacts are flagged as most recent for an account, o will contain multiple rows for that account_id. - Left join the daily rows to the account overview on account_id. If an account_id in daily has no matching overview, all profile fields are NULL; daily metrics remain as in rt. If overview has multiple rows for an account_id, the daily row is duplicated once per matching overview row. No additional filtering or transformations are applied.'
      columns:
      - name: account_id
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Zuora account identifier from transactions_grouped; join key to account profile.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_day
        data_type: DATE
        description: Calendar date for the daily metrics as provided by transactions_grouped; output includes only dates present in the spine.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: date_week
        data_type: DATE
        description: Week bucket corresponding to date_day as precomputed in transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: date_month
        data_type: DATE
        description: Month bucket corresponding to date_day as precomputed in transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: date_year
        data_type: DATE
        description: Year bucket corresponding to date_day as precomputed in transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: date_index
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Monotonic day index from the date spine for date_day; attached via inner join on date_day.
        tests:
        - not_null
      - name: account_created_at
        data_type: TIMESTAMP
        description: Account creation timestamp from account_current.created_date; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: account_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account name from account_current.name; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: account_number
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account number from account_current.account_number; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: account_status
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Account status from account_current.status; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: account_country
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Country from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact.
        tests: []
      - name: account_email
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Lowercased work_email from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact or work_email is NULL.
        tests: []
      - name: account_first_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: First name from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact.
        tests: []
      - name: account_last_name
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Last name from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact.
        tests: []
      - name: account_postal_code
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: Postal code from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact.
        tests: []
      - name: account_state
        data_type: VARCHAR
        description: State or region from the most recent account contact; NULL if no such contact.
        tests: []
      - name: first_charge_processed_at
        data_type: DATE
        description: Earliest invoice_date across all invoices for the account; NULL if the account has no invoices.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_invoices
        data_type: BIGINT
        description: Count of invoices on date_day from transactions_grouped; values are passed through unchanged and NULLs preserved.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_invoice_items
        data_type: HUGEINT
        description: Count of invoice line items on date_day from transactions_grouped; values are passed through unchanged and NULLs preserved.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_invoice_amount
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total invoiced amount on date_day from transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_invoice_amount_paid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total amount applied or paid on date_day from transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_invoice_amount_unpaid
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total outstanding or unpaid amount on date_day from transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []
      - name: daily_refunds
        data_type: DOUBLE
        description: Total refund amount on date_day from transactions_grouped; passed through unchanged.
        tests: []

## 命令原文
