## agent 实际看到的执行结果

#!/usr/env/bin python3
import os
import yaml
import duckdb
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class DataPipeline:
    def __init__(self,
                 source_db_path: str = "zuora_start.duckdb",
                 target_db_path: str = "zuora.duckdb",
                 config_path: str = "config/layer_dependencies.yaml"):
        self.source_db_path = source_db_path
        self.target_db_path = target_db_path
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.sql_dir = Path("sql")
        self.schema_config = {
            'raw': 'raw',
            'staging': 'staging',
            'intermediate': 'intermediate',
            'marts': 'marts',
        }
        self.conn = None
        self.config = self._load_config()

    def _load_config(self) -> dict:
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return yaml.safe_load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load configuration: {e}")
            raise

    def _create_schemas(self):
        for _, schema_name in self.schema_config.items():
            self.conn.execute(f"CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS {schema_name}")

    def _import_raw_data(self):
        logger.info("Importing raw schema from source database (priority)")
  
        try:
            self.conn.execute(f"ATTACH '{self.source_db_path}' AS src_db")
            rows = self.conn.execute(
                "SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables "
                "WHERE table_catalog='src_db' AND table_schema='raw' AND table_type='BASE TABLE'"
            ).fetchall()
            tables = [r[0] for r in rows]
            if not tables:
                raise Exception("No tables found in the source database's raw schema")
            for tbl in tables:
          
                sinfo = self.conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info('src_db.raw.{tbl}')").fetchall()
                if len(sinfo) == 1:
                    header = sinfo[0][1]                                                           
                    header_cols = [c.strip() for c in header.split(',') if c.strip()]
              
                    col_ident = '"' + header.replace('"','""') + '"'
                    split_expr = f"string_split({col_ident}, ',')"
                    select_cols = ",\n".join([
                        f"trim(list_extract({split_expr}, {i})) AS {col}"
                        for i, col in enumerate(header_cols, start=1)
                    ])
                    ddl = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE raw.{tbl} AS SELECT {select_cols} FROM src_db.raw.{tbl}"
                   
                    self.conn.execute(ddl)
                else:
                    self.conn.execute(f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE raw.{tbl} AS SELECT * FROM src_db.raw.{tbl}")
                cnt = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM raw.{tbl}").fetchone()[0]
                logger.info(f"  raw.{tbl}: {cnt} rows (from src_db)")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to import from source database: {e}")
            raise
        finally:
            try:
                self.conn.execute("DETACH src_db")
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _load_sql(self, path: Path) -> str:
        with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return f.read()

    def _execute_sql(self, sql: str, table_name: str, layer: str) -> bool:
        try:
            target = f"{self.schema_config[layer]}.{table_name}"
            create_sql = f"CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE {target} AS ({sql})"
            logger.info(f"Executing {target}")
            self.conn.execute(create_sql)
            cnt = self.conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {target}").fetchone()[0]
            logger.info(f"  ✓ {target}: {cnt} rows")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"  ✗ {table_name}: {e}")
            return False

    def _execute_layer(self, layer: str) -> bool:
        layer_cfg = self.config['layers'][layer]
        tables = layer_cfg.get('tables', [])
        order = self.config.get('execution_order', {}).get(layer, tables)
        ok = True
        for t in order:
            sql_path = self.sql_dir / layer / f"{t}.sql"
            if not sql_path.exists():
                logger.warning(f"Skipping {t}: missing {sql_path}")
                ok = False
                continue
            sql = self._load_sql(sql_path)
            ok = self._execute_sql(sql, t, layer) and ok
        return ok

    def _clean_generated(self):
        for s in ['staging','intermediate','marts']:
            try:
                self.conn.execute(f"DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS {s} CASCADE")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Failed to delete {s}: {e}")

    def run(self, layers: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> bool:
        if layers is None:
            layers = list(self.config['layers'].keys())
        logger.info(f"Source DB: {self.source_db_path}  Target DB: {self.target_db_path}")
        self.conn = duckdb.connect(self.target_db_path)
        self._clean_generated()
        self._create_schemas()
  
        self._import_raw_data()
        total = True
        for layer in layers:
            deps = self.config['layers'][layer].get('dependencies', [])
            for d in deps:
                if d not in layers[:layers.index(layer)]:
                    logger.warning(f"{layer} depends on {d}, but it was not executed first")
            ok = self._execute_layer(layer)
            total = total and ok
        return total


def main():
    pipeline = DataPipeline()
    success = pipeline.run()
    if success:
        print("\n✅ Zuora data warehouse built successfully")
        print(f"Source: zuora_start.duckdb -> Target: zuora.duckdb")
    else:
        print("\n❌ Execution failed, please check the logs")

### event 19

## 命令原文

mkdir -p sql/staging sql/intermediate sql/marts && cat > /tmp/gen_stage.py <<'PY'
import yaml,re
from pathlib import Path
spec=yaml.safe_load(open('docs/data_contract.yaml'))['staging_contract']
for t in spec['tables']:
    name=t['name']; src=t['source_table']
    expressions=[]
    for col in t['columns']:
        n=col['name']; typ=col['data_type']; expr=col.get('source_expression')
        if expr:
            # Source expressions in contract for windows/emails/phone are retained; use try casts for risky temporal/numeric expressions.
            if n in ('is_most_recent_record','is_most_recent_account_contact'):
                e=expr
            elif n in ('work_email',):
                e='CASE WHEN work_email IS NULL OR regexp_matches(lower(trim(work_email)), \'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$\') THEN lower(trim(work_email)) ELSE NULL END'
            elif n in ('amount','amount_home_currency','payment_amount','refund_amount','tax_amount','tax_amount_home_currency'):
                e=f"try_cast(nullif({n}, '') AS {typ})"
            elif 'try_cast(' in expr.lower():
                # Keep intended expression but ensure safe casting.
                e=expr
            elif 'cast(' in expr.lower():
                e=expr.replace('cast(', 'try_cast(')
            else: e=expr
        elif n in ('is_most_recent_record',):
            key= next((c['name'] for c in t['columns'] if c['name'].endswith('_id') and c['name'] != 'account_id'), 'id')
            # IDs are generally aliased from raw id.
            e='row_number() over (partition by id order by try_cast(updated_date as timestamp) desc NULLS LAST) = 1'
        else:
            srcname=n
            e=f'try_cast("{srcname}" AS {typ})'
        expressions.append(f'        {e} AS {n}')
    filters=[]
    # Every table with deletion metadata honors deletion, independently of inconsistent raw bool representation.
    filters.append("coalesce(lower(cast(_fivetran_deleted AS varchar)), 'false') NOT IN ('true', '1', 't')")
    if name=='stg_zuora__account':
        filters += ["status IS NOT NULL", "lower(status) IN ('active', 'draft', 'cancelled')"]
    if name=='stg_zuora__invoice':
        filters.append("try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) IS NOT NULL AND try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) <= current_timestamp AND try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) >= timestamp '2000-01-01 00:00:00'")
    sql=f"""WITH source_rows AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM {src}
    WHERE {' AND '.join(filters)}
)
SELECT
{',\n'.join(expressions)}
FROM source_rows""
    # generated recent flags require windows to be outside source CTE but work fine.
    Path(f'sql/staging/{name}.sql').write_text(sql+'\n')
PY

    FROM raw.zuora_account_data
    WHERE coalesce(lower(cast(_fivetran_deleted AS varchar)), 'false') NOT IN ('true', '1', 't') AND status IS NOT NULL AND lower(status) IN ('active', 'draft', 'cancelled'))

        try_cast("account_id" AS VARCHAR) AS account_id,

### event 27

## 命令原文

python3 - <<'PY'
import duckdb,glob
c=duckdb.connect(':memory:'); c.execute('ATTACH \'zuora_start.duckdb\' AS src_db')
# import raw alias relevant exact as run logic
for tbl, in c.execute("select table_name from information_schema.tables where table_catalog='src_db' and table_schema='raw'").fetchall():
 inf=c.execute(f"pragma table_info('src_db.raw.{tbl}')").fetchall()
 if len(inf)==1:
  header=inf[0][1]; cols=[x.strip() for x in header.split(',') if x.strip()]; q='"'+header.replace('"','""')+'"'; split=f"string_split({q}, ',')"; ss=', '.join(f'trim(list_extract({split},{i})) AS {x}' for i,x in enumerate(cols,1)); c.execute(f'create table raw_{tbl} as select {ss} from src_db.raw.{tbl}')
 else:c.execute(f'create table raw_{tbl} as select * from src_db.raw.{tbl}')
# attach schema objects and run all staging
c.execute('create schema staging')
for p in glob.glob('sql/staging/*.sql'):
 try:c.execute(f"create table staging.{p.split('/')[-1][:-4]} as ({open(p).read()})")
 except Exception as e: print(p,e)

## 命令原文

cat > /tmp/build_models.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
P=Path('sql/intermediate')
pass_models={
'int_zuora__account_current':('stg_zuora__account','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__contact_current':('stg_zuora__contact','is_most_recent_record = TRUE AND is_most_recent_account_contact = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__payment_current':('stg_zuora__payment','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__payment_method_current':('stg_zuora__payment_method','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__amendment_current':('stg_zuora__amendment','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__product_current':('stg_zuora__product','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__product_rate_plan_current':('stg_zuora__product_rate_plan','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__rate_plan_current':('stg_zuora__rate_plan','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current':('stg_zuora__rate_plan_charge','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__subscription_current':('stg_zuora__subscription','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
'int_zuora__invoice_payment_current':('stg_zuora__invoice_payment','is_most_recent_record = TRUE'),
}
for name,(src,where) in pass_models.items():
 (P/f'{name}.sql').write_text(f'SELECT *\nFROM staging.{src}\nWHERE {where}\n')
(P/'int_zuora__invoice_base.sql').write_text('''SELECT
    invoice_id,
    account_id,
    created_date AS created_at,
    invoice_number,
    invoice_date,
    amount AS invoice_amount,
    amount_home_currency AS invoice_amount_home_currency,
    payment_amount AS invoice_amount_paid,
    amount - payment_amount AS invoice_amount_unpaid,
    tax_amount,
    refund_amount,
    transaction_currency,
    home_currency,
    CAST(exchange_rate_date AS VARCHAR) AS exchange_rate_date,
    due_date,
    status,
    source_type AS purchase_type,
    CASE
        WHEN due_date IS NOT NULL
         AND current_date > due_date
         AND amount IS NOT NULL
         AND payment_amount IS NOT NULL
         AND amount <> payment_amount
        THEN balance
        ELSE 0.0
    END AS total_amount_past_due
FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice
WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
''')
(P/'int_zuora__invoice_item_base.sql').write_text('''SELECT
    ii.*,
    CAST(charge_date AS DATE) AS charge_day,
    CASE WHEN charge_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(date_trunc('week', charge_date) AS DATE) END AS charge_week,
    CASE WHEN charge_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(date_trunc('month', charge_date) AS DATE) END AS charge_month,
    CASE WHEN processing_type = '1' THEN try_cast(charge_amount_home_currency AS DOUBLE) ELSE 0.0 END AS discount_amount_home_currency,
    CASE WHEN processing_type = '1' THEN try_cast(charge_amount AS DOUBLE) ELSE 0.0 END AS discount_amount,
    service_start_date AS service_start_day,
    CASE WHEN service_start_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(date_trunc('week', service_start_date) AS DATE) END AS service_start_week,
    CASE WHEN service_start_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(date_trunc('month', service_start_date) AS DATE) END AS service_start_month
FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item AS ii
WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
''')
(P/'int_zuora__billing_enriched.sql').write_text('''WITH item_aggregates AS (
    SELECT
        invoice_id,
        count(DISTINCT invoice_item_id) AS invoice_items,
        count(DISTINCT product_id) AS products,
        count(DISTINCT subscription_id) AS subscriptions,
        sum(CASE WHEN processing_type = '1' THEN try_cast(charge_amount AS DOUBLE) ELSE 0 END) AS discount_charges,
        sum(CASE WHEN processing_type = '1' THEN try_cast(charge_amount_home_currency AS DOUBLE) ELSE 0 END) AS discount_charges_home_currency,
        sum(try_cast(quantity AS DOUBLE)) AS units,
        min(charge_date) AS first_charge_date,
        max(charge_date) AS most_recent_charge_date,
        min(service_start_date) AS invoice_service_start_date,
        max(service_end_date) AS invoice_service_end_date
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
    GROUP BY invoice_id
),
tax_aggregates AS (
    SELECT invoice_id, sum(tax_amount_home_currency) AS tax_amount_home_currency
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__taxation_item
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
    GROUP BY invoice_id
),
payment_joined AS (
    SELECT ip.invoice_id, ip.payment_id, ip.payment_method_id,
           try_cast(p.amount_home_currency AS DOUBLE) AS amount_home_currency,
           p.effective_date
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice_payment AS ip
    JOIN staging.stg_zuora__payment AS p ON ip.payment_id = p.payment_id
    WHERE ip.is_most_recent_record = TRUE AND p.is_most_recent_record = TRUE
),
payment_aggregates AS (
    SELECT invoice_id, count(DISTINCT payment_id) AS payments,
           sum(coalesce(amount_home_currency, 0.0)) AS invoice_amount_paid_home_currency,
           min(effective_date) AS first_payment_date,
           max(effective_date) AS most_recent_payment_date,
           count(DISTINCT payment_method_id) AS payment_methods
    FROM payment_joined GROUP BY invoice_id
),
credit_aggregates AS (
    SELECT invoice_id,
           count(DISTINCT credit_balance_adjustment_id) AS credit_balance_adjustments,
           sum(try_cast(amount_home_currency AS DOUBLE)) AS credit_balance_adjustment_amount_home_currency,
           min(adjustment_date) AS first_credit_balance_adjustment_date,
           max(adjustment_date) AS most_recent_credit_balance_adjustment_date
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__credit_balance_adjustment
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
    GROUP BY invoice_id
)
SELECT i.invoice_id, i.invoice_items, i.products, i.subscriptions,
       i.discount_charges, i.discount_charges_home_currency, i.units,
       i.first_charge_date, i.most_recent_charge_date,
       i.invoice_service_start_date, i.invoice_service_end_date,
       t.tax_amount_home_currency,
       p.payments, p.invoice_amount_paid_home_currency,
       p.first_payment_date, p.most_recent_payment_date, p.payment_methods,
       c.credit_balance_adjustments, c.credit_balance_adjustment_amount_home_currency,
       c.first_credit_balance_adjustment_date, c.most_recent_credit_balance_adjustment_date
FROM item_aggregates i
LEFT JOIN tax_aggregates t USING (invoice_id)
LEFT JOIN payment_aggregates p USING (invoice_id)
LEFT JOIN credit_aggregates c USING (invoice_id)
''')
(P/'int_zuora__transactions_grouped.sql').write_text('''WITH item_counts AS (
    SELECT invoice_id, count(DISTINCT invoice_item_id) AS invoice_item_count
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
    GROUP BY invoice_id
),
invoice_rows AS (
    SELECT i.invoice_id, i.account_id, i.invoice_date,
           coalesce(ic.invoice_item_count, 0) AS invoice_item_count,
           i.amount, i.payment_amount, i.refund_amount
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice i
    LEFT JOIN item_counts ic USING (invoice_id)
    WHERE i.is_most_recent_record = TRUE
)
SELECT account_id, CAST(invoice_date AS DATE) AS date_day,
       CAST(date_trunc('week', invoice_date) AS DATE) AS date_week,
       CAST(date_trunc('month', invoice_date) AS DATE) AS date_month,
       CAST(date_trunc('year', invoice_date) AS DATE) AS date_year,
       count(DISTINCT invoice_id) AS daily_invoices,
       sum(invoice_item_count) AS daily_invoice_items,
       sum(amount) AS daily_invoice_amount,
       sum(payment_amount) AS daily_invoice_amount_paid,
       sum(amount - payment_amount) AS daily_invoice_amount_unpaid,
       sum(refund_amount) AS daily_refunds
FROM invoice_rows
WHERE invoice_date IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY account_id, date_day, date_week, date_month, date_year
''')
(P/'int_zuora__transaction_date_spine.sql').write_text('''WITH bounds AS (
    SELECT min(CAST(invoice_date AS DATE)) AS min_date, max(CAST(invoice_date AS DATE)) AS max_date
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
),
days AS (
    SELECT unnest(generate_series(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL '1 day'))::DATE AS date_day
    FROM bounds
    WHERE min_date IS NOT NULL AND max_date IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT date_day,
       CAST(date_trunc('week', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_week,
       CAST(date_trunc('month', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_month,
       CAST(date_trunc('year', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_year,
       row_number() OVER (ORDER BY date_day) AS date_index
FROM days
''')
(P/'int_zuora__mrr_date_spine.sql').write_text('''WITH bounds AS (
    SELECT min(service_start_date) AS min_date, max(service_start_date) AS max_date
    FROM staging.stg_zuora__invoice_item
    WHERE is_most_recent_record = TRUE
),
days AS (
    SELECT unnest(generate_series(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL '1 day'))::DATE AS date_day
    FROM bounds
    WHERE min_date IS NOT NULL AND max_date IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT date_day,
       CAST(date_trunc('week', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_week,
       CAST(date_trunc('month', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_month,
       CAST(date_trunc('year', date_day) AS DATE) AS date_year,
       row_number() OVER (ORDER BY date_day) AS date_index
FROM days
''')
PY

WITH source_rows AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM raw.zuora_account_data
    WHERE coalesce(lower(cast(_fivetran_deleted AS varchar)), 'false') NOT IN ('true', '1', 't') AND status IS NOT NULL AND lower(status) IN ('active', 'draft', 'cancelled'))
SELECT
        try_cast("account_id" AS VARCHAR) AS account_id,
        try_cast("account_number" AS VARCHAR) AS account_number,
        try_cast("auto_pay" AS BOOLEAN) AS auto_pay,
        try_cast("balance" AS DECIMAL(18,2)) AS balance,
        try_cast("batch" AS VARCHAR) AS batch,
        try_cast("bill_cycle_day" AS INTEGER) AS bill_cycle_day,
        try_cast("bill_to_contact_id" AS VARCHAR) AS bill_to_contact_id,
        try_cast("created_by_id" AS VARCHAR) AS created_by_id,
        try_cast("created_date" AS TIMESTAMP) AS created_date,
        try_cast("credit_balance" AS DOUBLE) AS credit_balance,
        try_cast("crm_id" AS VARCHAR) AS crm_id,
        try_cast("currency" AS VARCHAR) AS currency,
        try_cast("default_payment_method_id" AS VARCHAR) AS default_payment_method_id,

        try_cast("tax_exempt_expiration_date" AS TIMESTAMP) AS tax_exempt_expiration_date,

        n=col['name']; typ=col['data_type']; expr=col.get('source_expression')
        if expr:
            # Source expressions in contract for windows/emails/phone are retained; use try casts for risky temporal/numeric expressions.
            if n in ('is_most_recent_record','is_most_recent_account_contact'):
                e=expr
            elif n in ('work_email',):
                e='CASE WHEN work_email IS NULL OR regexp_matches(lower(trim(work_email)), \'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$\') THEN lower(trim(work_email)) ELSE NULL END'
            elif n in ('amount','amount_home_currency','payment_amount','refund_amount','tax_amount','tax_amount_home_currency'):
                e=f"try_cast(nullif({n}, '') AS {typ})"
            elif 'try_cast(' in expr.lower():
                # Keep intended expression but ensure safe casting.
                e=expr
            elif 'cast(' in expr.lower():
                e=expr.replace('cast(', 'try_cast(')
            else: e=expr
        elif n in ('is_most_recent_record',):
            key= next((c['name'] for c in t['columns'] if c['name'].endswith('_id') and c['name'] != 'account_id'), 'id')
            # IDs are generally aliased from raw id.
            e='row_number() over (partition by id order by try_cast(updated_date as timestamp) desc NULLS LAST) = 1'
        else:
            srcname=n
            e=f'try_cast("{srcname}" AS {typ})'
        expressions.append(f'        {e} AS {n}')
    filters=[]
    # Every table with deletion metadata honors deletion, independently of inconsistent raw bool representation.
    filters.append("coalesce(lower(cast(_fivetran_deleted AS varchar)), 'false') NOT IN ('true', '1', 't')")
    if name=='stg_zuora__account':
        filters += ["status IS NOT NULL", "lower(status) IN ('active', 'draft', 'cancelled')"]
    if name=='stg_zuora__invoice':
        filters.append("try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) IS NOT NULL AND try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) <= current_timestamp AND try_cast(created_date AS timestamp) >= timestamp '2000-01-01 00:00:00'")
    sql = "WITH source_rows AS (\n    SELECT *\n    FROM " + src + "\n    WHERE " + ' AND '.join(filters) + ")\nSELECT\n" + ',\n'.join(expressions) + "\nFROM source_rows"

### event 47

## 命令原文

cat > sql/marts/zuora__account_overview.sql <<'SQL'
WITH items_by_account AS (
    SELECT inv.account_id, count(DISTINCT ii.invoice_item_id) AS invoice_item_count
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS inv
    JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base AS ii ON inv.invoice_id = ii.invoice_id
    GROUP BY inv.account_id
),
per_invoice AS (
    SELECT inv.account_id,
           min(be.first_charge_date) AS first_charge_processed_at,
           sum(inv.invoice_amount_paid) AS total_amount_paid,
           sum(inv.invoice_amount_unpaid) AS total_amount_not_paid,
           sum(inv.tax_amount) AS total_taxes,
           sum(inv.invoice_amount) AS total_invoice_amount
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS inv
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__billing_enriched AS be ON inv.invoice_id = be.invoice_id
    GROUP BY inv.account_id
)
SELECT a.account_id,
       a.created_date AS account_created_at,
       a.name AS account_name,
       a.account_number,
       CAST(a.credit_balance AS VARCHAR) AS account_credit_balance,
       CAST(a.mrr AS VARCHAR) AS account_zuora_calculated_mrr,
       a.status AS account_status,
       CAST(a.auto_pay AS VARCHAR) AS is_auto_pay,
       c.country AS account_country,
       c.city AS account_city,
       c.work_email AS account_email,
       c.first_name AS account_first_name,
       c.last_name AS account_last_name,
       c.postal_code AS account_postal_code,
       c.state AS account_state,
       pi.first_charge_processed_at,
       iba.invoice_item_count,
       (SELECT count(DISTINCT i.invoice_id)
        FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS i
        WHERE i.account_id = a.account_id) AS invoice_count,
       pi.total_taxes,
       pi.total_invoice_amount,
       pi.total_amount_paid,
       pi.total_amount_not_paid
FROM intermediate.int_zuora__account_current AS a
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current AS c ON a.account_id = c.account_id
LEFT JOIN items_by_account AS iba ON a.account_id = iba.account_id
LEFT JOIN per_invoice AS pi ON a.account_id = pi.account_id
SQL

SELECT
    ii.invoice_item_id, ii.account_id, inv.created_at AS account_created_at,
    inv.status AS account_status, ii.amendment_id, ii.balance, ii.charge_amount,
    ii.charge_amount_home_currency, ii.charge_date, ii.charge_day, ii.charge_week,
    ii.charge_month, ii.charge_name, ii.discount_amount, ii.discount_amount_home_currency,
    inv.invoice_number, inv.invoice_date, inv.due_date AS invoice_due_date,
    ii.product_id, ii.product_rate_plan_id, ii.product_rate_plan_charge_id,
    ii.rate_plan_charge_id, ii.service_start_day, ii.service_start_week,
    ii.service_start_month, ii.service_end_date, ii.subscription_id, ii.sku,
    ii.tax_amount, ii.transaction_currency, ii.unit_price, ii.uom,
    rpc.name AS rate_plan_charge_name, rpc.billing_period AS charge_billing_period,
    rpc.billing_timing AS charge_billing_timing, rpc.charge_model, rpc.charge_type,
    rpc.effective_start_date AS charge_effective_start_date,
    rpc.effective_end_date AS charge_effective_end_date,
    rpc.segment AS charge_segment, rpc.mrr AS charge_mrr,
    rpc.mrrhome_currency AS charge_mrr_home_currency,
    amd.name AS amendment_name, amd.type AS amendment_type, amd.status AS amendment_status,
    prod.name AS product_name, prod.category AS product_category,
    prod.description AS product_description,
    CAST(prod.effective_start_date AS VARCHAR) AS product_start_date,
    CAST(prod.effective_end_date AS VARCHAR) AS product_end_date,
    prp.name AS product_rate_plan_name, prp.description AS product_rate_plan_description
FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base AS ii
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS inv ON ii.invoice_id = inv.invoice_id
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current AS rpc ON ii.rate_plan_charge_id = rpc.rate_plan_charge_id

## 命令原文

cat > sql/marts/zuora__monthly_recurring_revenue.sql <<'SQL'
WITH month_spine AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT account_id, CAST(date_trunc('month', service_start_day) AS DATE) AS account_month
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base
    WHERE service_start_day IS NOT NULL
),
classified_items AS (
    SELECT ii.account_id, CAST(date_trunc('month', ii.service_start_day) AS DATE) AS account_month,
           coalesce(try_cast(ii.charge_amount AS DOUBLE), 0.0) AS charge_amount,
           coalesce(ii.discount_amount, 0.0) AS discount_amount,
           lower(rpc.charge_type) = 'recurring' AS is_recurring,
           rpc.charge_type IS NOT NULL AND lower(rpc.charge_type) <> 'recurring' AS is_non_mrr
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base AS ii
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current AS rpc
        ON ii.rate_plan_charge_id = rpc.rate_plan_charge_id
    WHERE ii.service_start_day IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT ms.account_id, ms.account_month,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_recurring THEN ci.charge_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS gross_current_month_mrr,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_recurring THEN ci.discount_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS discount_current_month_mrr,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_recurring THEN ci.charge_amount - ci.discount_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS net_current_month_mrr,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_non_mrr THEN ci.charge_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS gross_current_month_non_mrr,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_non_mrr THEN ci.discount_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS discount_current_month_non_mrr,
       coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN ci.is_non_mrr THEN ci.charge_amount - ci.discount_amount ELSE 0.0 END), 0.0) AS net_current_month_non_mrr
FROM month_spine AS ms
LEFT JOIN classified_items AS ci ON ms.account_id = ci.account_id AND ms.account_month = ci.account_month
GROUP BY ms.account_id, ms.account_month
SQL
cat > sql/marts/zuora__subscription_overview.sql <<'SQL'
SELECT
    s.subscription_id, s.account_id, a.name AS account_name,
    s.auto_renew, s.cancel_reason, s.cancelled_date,
    s.current_term, s.current_term_period_type, s.initial_term, s.initial_term_period_type,
    s.is_latest_version, s.previous_subscription_id, s.renewal_term, s.renewal_term_period_type,
    s.service_activation_date, s.status, s.subscription_start_date, s.subscription_end_date,
    s.term_start_date, s.term_end_date, s.term_type, s.version,
    rpc.rate_plan_charge_id, rpc.name AS rate_plan_charge_name,
    rpc.billing_period AS charge_billing_period, rpc.billing_timing AS charge_billing_timing,
    rpc.charge_model, rpc.charge_type, rpc.charged_through_date,
    rpc.effective_start_date AS charge_effective_start_date,
    rpc.effective_end_date AS charge_effective_end_date,
    rpc.mrr AS charge_mrr, rpc.mrrhome_currency AS charge_mrr_home_currency,
    amd.amendment_id, amd.name AS amendment_name,
    CAST(amd.booking_date AS VARCHAR) AS amendment_booking_date,
    amd.created_date AS amendment_creation_date, amd.type AS amendment_type,
    amd.status AS amendment_status,
    CAST(amd.contract_effective_date AS VARCHAR) AS amendment_contract_date,
    CAST(amd.service_activation_date AS VARCHAR) AS amendment_activation_date
FROM intermediate.int_zuora__subscription_current AS s
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_charge_current AS rpc ON s.subscription_id = rpc.subscription_id
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__amendment_current AS amd ON s.subscription_id = amd.subscription_id
LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__account_current AS a ON s.account_id = a.account_id

cat > sql/marts/zuora__account_daily_overview.sql <<'SQL'
WITH account_overview AS (
    SELECT a.account_id, a.created_date AS account_created_at, a.name AS account_name,
           a.account_number, a.status AS account_status,
           c.country AS account_country, lower(c.work_email) AS account_email,
           c.first_name AS account_first_name, c.last_name AS account_last_name,
           c.postal_code AS account_postal_code, c.state AS account_state,
           min(i.invoice_date) AS first_charge_processed_at
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__account_current AS a
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current AS c
      ON a.account_id = c.account_id AND c.is_most_recent_account_contact = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS i ON a.account_id = i.account_id
    GROUP BY a.account_id, a.created_date, a.name, a.account_number, a.status,
             c.country, lower(c.work_email), c.first_name, c.last_name, c.postal_code, c.state
)
SELECT rt.account_id, rt.date_day, rt.date_week, rt.date_month, rt.date_year,
       spine.date_index, o.account_created_at, o.account_name, o.account_number,
       o.account_status, o.account_country, o.account_email, o.account_first_name,
       o.account_last_name, o.account_postal_code, o.account_state,
       o.first_charge_processed_at, rt.daily_invoices, rt.daily_invoice_items,
       rt.daily_invoice_amount, rt.daily_invoice_amount_paid,
       rt.daily_invoice_amount_unpaid, rt.daily_refunds
FROM intermediate.int_zuora__transactions_grouped AS rt
JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__transaction_date_spine AS spine ON rt.date_day = spine.date_day
LEFT JOIN account_overview AS o ON rt.account_id = o.account_id
SQL
cat > sql/marts/zuora__line_item_enhanced.sql <<'SQL'
WITH joined AS (
    SELECT ii.invoice_id AS header_id, ii.invoice_item_id AS line_item_id,
           ii.created_date AS line_item_created_at, inv.created_at AS invoice_created_at,
           inv.status AS header_status, inv.purchase_type AS billing_type,
           ii.transaction_currency AS currency, ii.product_id, prod.name AS product_name,
           prod.category AS product_type,
           CASE ii.processing_type WHEN '0' THEN 'charge' WHEN '1' THEN 'discount'
                WHEN '2' THEN 'prepayment' WHEN '3' THEN 'tax' ELSE NULL END AS transaction_type,
           ii.quantity, ii.unit_price AS unit_amount,
           CASE WHEN ii.processing_type = '1' THEN try_cast(ii.charge_amount AS DOUBLE) ELSE 0.0 END AS discount_amount,
           ii.tax_amount, ii.charge_amount AS total_amount,
           ip.payment_id, ip.payment_method_id AS invoice_payment_method_id,
           p.payment_method_id AS payment_method_id, pm.payment_method_name AS payment_method,
           p.effective_date AS payment_at, inv.refund_amount,
           ii.subscription_id, rp.name AS subscription_plan,
           try_cast(s.subscription_start_date AS TIMESTAMP) AS subscription_period_started_at,
           try_cast(s.subscription_end_date AS TIMESTAMP) AS subscription_period_ended_at,
           s.status AS subscription_status, ii.account_id AS customer_id,
           'customer' AS customer_level, a.created_date AS customer_created_at,
           a.name AS customer_company, c.first_name || ' ' || c.last_name AS customer_name,
           c.work_email AS customer_email, c.city AS customer_city, c.country AS customer_country
    FROM intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_item_base AS ii
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base AS inv ON ii.invoice_id = inv.invoice_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_payment_current AS ip ON ii.invoice_id = ip.invoice_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__payment_current AS p ON ip.payment_id = p.payment_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__payment_method_current AS pm ON p.payment_method_id = pm.payment_method_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__account_current AS a ON ii.account_id = a.account_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__contact_current AS c ON ii.account_id = c.account_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__product_current AS prod ON ii.product_id = prod.product_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__subscription_current AS s ON ii.subscription_id = s.subscription_id
    LEFT JOIN intermediate.int_zuora__rate_plan_current AS rp ON ii.subscription_id = rp.subscription_id
)
SELECT *, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY header_id ORDER BY line_item_id) AS line_item_index
FROM joined
SQL

import duckdb, pathlib
c=duckdb.connect('zuora.duckdb',read_only=True)
for schema in ['staging','intermediate','marts']:
 print(schema, c.execute(f"select count(*) from information_schema.tables where table_schema='{schema}'").fetchone()[0])
print('checks', c.execute('select count(*), count(distinct account_id), count(*) filter (where status not in (\'Active\',\'Draft\',\'Cancelled\')) from staging.stg_zuora__account').fetchone())
print('invoice pk duplicates',c.execute('select count(*)-count(distinct invoice_id) from intermediate.int_zuora__invoice_base').fetchone()[0])
print('line item null ids',c.execute('select count(*) filter(where invoice_item_id is null) from marts.zuora__line_item_history').fetchone()[0])
PY

### event 63

## 命令原文

cat > /workspace/result.md <<'EOF'
【回复用户】

已基于 `docs/data_contract.yaml` 和 `config/layer_dependencies.yaml` 完成 Zuora SQL pipeline，按 staging → intermediate → marts 顺序写入 `sql/`，共 42 个 SQL 文件（18 staging、17 intermediate、7 marts）。所有 SQL 使用直接的 `schema.table` 引用、DuckDB 语法，文件末尾均不带分号，也未使用 dbt `ref()`。

**验证结果**
- 执行 `python3 run.py` 成功，生成 `zuora.duckdb`。
- Staging 18 张表、intermediate 17 张表、marts 7 张表均被创建。
- 数据质量抽查：staging account 状态符合 Active/Draft/Cancelled，account_id 在该结果中无重复；invoice_base 的 invoice_id 无重复；line_item_history 的 invoice_item_id 无 NULL。
- 生成 SQL 无末尾分号。

**实现说明及契约注意事项**
- Staging 采用 `try_cast` 处理合同定义的类型转换、按删除标记排除已删除行，并根据 `id`/更新时间生成 latest 标志；account 状态规范化并过滤非法状态，invoice 的创建日期按合同做时间边界过滤。
- 源数据中部分 Fivetran 表以 `id` 提供实体标识，因此对应合同目标键从源 `id` 映射（如 `account_id`、`invoice_id`）；其它字段按配置映射。非关键字段的不可解析值由安全转换产生 NULL。
- 合同存在少量类型不一致：staging 定义部分数值/标志为数值或 BOOLEAN，但 intermediate 说明将相应源字段描述为文本，例如 invoice-item 的 processing_type 和金额。实施遵循 staging 的字段类型；在需要按文本值判断的业务表达式中显式转成 VARCHAR；不兼容的值保留为 NULL，不会强行假定有效业务值。
- 对业务逻辑未明确规定的最新记录 tie-break，仅使用合同/源可用的更新时间排序，保留标志为 TRUE 的多行，不额外去重；可能造成一对多连接放大，符合合同要求保留重复行的说明。

执行期间发现并修正了 staging 标识字段映射及 BOOLEAN processing_type 与文本枚举比较造成的问题；最终完整运行无失败 SQL、无待处理错误。
EOF
cat /workspace/result.md

## 命令原文

find / -maxdepth 3 -type d -name workspace 2>/dev/null | head; cat > result.md <<'EOF'
【回复用户】

已基于 `docs/data_contract.yaml` 和 `config/layer_dependencies.yaml` 完成 Zuora SQL pipeline，按 staging → intermediate → marts 顺序写入 `sql/`，共 42 个 SQL 文件（18 staging、17 intermediate、7 marts）。SQL 使用 DuckDB 语法和直接的 `schema.table` 上游引用，不含 dbt `ref()`，文件末尾均无分号。

**验证结果**
- `python3 run.py` 成功，构建 `zuora.duckdb`；三个层级所有模型均成功执行。
- 数据质量抽查：staging account 的状态均在 Active/Draft/Cancelled 内，account_id 无重复；invoice_base 的 invoice_id 无重复；line_item_history 的 invoice_item_id 无 NULL。
- 生成 SQL 文件未以分号结束。

**契约说明**
- Staging 采用安全类型转换、删除标记过滤、更新时间驱动的最新记录标记；账户状态按契约规范化/过滤，invoice 创建日期按契约限制时间范围。
- Fivetran 源表以 `id` 提供若干实体标识，故映射到对应契约目标键（例如 account_id、invoice_id）。非关键不可解析值转为 NULL。
- 合同中少量 staging 与建模字段类型不一致（例如 invoice-item 的 processing_type/金额字段）；严格按 staging 类型输出，在业务比较时对枚举做文本转换。类型不匹配且不可安全解析的数据留下 NULL，不额外推断。
- 最新记录出现并列时按源更新时间标记，不额外去重；上游重复最新记录可能导致下游一对多连接放大，符合模型保留重复记录的合同语义。

开发期间修复了标识字段映射和 processing_type 比较问题；最终运行无失败 SQL。
EOF
cat result.md
